- **multiple-testing-correction-application**: Use when when performing statistical tests (e.g., t-tests, ANOVA) across many metabolites in a MultiAssayExperiment object to identify differentially abundant metabolites between sample groups. - **multiple-testing-correction-fdr**: Use when testing many motifs or functional terms simultaneously — e.g. PASTAA on REM sequences ranked by Cell type score, or g:Profiler on ~1,721 ARID3A-associated genes — to control the false discovery rate at BH FDR ≤ 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-fdr))
- **multiple-testing-correction-fdr**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics feature table (CSV) has been tested feature-by-feature (t-test/ANOVA, or covariate-adjusted limma/ANCOVA models) and the resulting raw p-values must be FDR-corrected to define significant feature sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-fdr))
- **multiple-testing-correction-for-genomics**: Use when a per-gene statistical test (e.g., Wilcoxon rank-sum between cell clusters) returns thousands of raw p-values that must be multiple-testing corrected before declaring significant DE genes or enrichment terms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-for-genomics))
- **multiple-testing-correction-function-implementation**: Use when you need Meff estimates for eigen-analysis-based multiple testing correction: you have M correlated tests (e.g., metabolites) and a Pearson correlation matrix, and must compute effective numbers of tests for p-value adjustment as in DisCo P-ad. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-function-implementation))
- **multiple-testing-correction-local-vs-global**: Use when benchmarking or choosing the scope of FDR control (per-cluster vs. global BH correction) for gene-subpopulation differential state tests produced by muscat pbDS/mmDS across multiple samples and conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-local-vs-global))
- **multiple-testing-correction-metabolomics**: Use when you have computed raw p-values from partial Spearman correlations (or other univariate tests) between each metabolite in a SummarizedExperiment object and a phenotype of interest, adjusted for epidemiological confounders (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-metabolomics))
- **multiple-testing-correction-of-enrichment-pvalues**: Use when annotating clusters from a marker gene list with deCS.fisher: per-cell-type Fisher tests yield many raw p-values that must be corrected (e.g. bonferroni) before calling significant cell type assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-of-enrichment-pvalues))
- **multiple-testing-correction-pvalues**: Use when many hypothesis tests are run in parallel (e.g., scoring 64 latent features for association with ICB responder vs non-responder groups) and per-feature significance must be assessed with adjusted p-values to control false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-pvalues))
- **multiple-testing-correction-simulation**: Use when evaluating whether an alternative correlation matrix (e.g., Spearman's rank correlation) improves or matches eigen-analysis-based Meff estimates in a simulation scenario such as a small sample size (n=100). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-simulation))
- **multiple-testing-correction-statistics**: Use when comparing two cell groups (e.g. +FA benign vs +FA+IL17A inflammatory steatotic hepatocytes) across hundreds of metabolites in a per-cell matrix and uncorrected p-values would inflate false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-statistics))
- **multiple-testing-correction-thresholding**: Use when calling modified transcriptomic sites (m6A/m5C k-mers) from nanopore direct RNA predictions across a large number of sites, and per-site significance or probability must be corrected at alpha = 0.05, 0.01, or 0.001. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-correction-thresholding))
- **multiple-testing-correction-visualization**: Use when after running fgsea() on a preranked gene list and obtaining a results table with raw pval, padj, ES, NES, and size columns, use this skill to: (1) subset results to top enriched pathways (e.g., top 10 upregulated ES &gt; 0 and top 10 downregulated ES &lt; 0 by adjusted p-value); ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/multiple-testing-correction-visualization))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-bonferroni-tuning**: Use when running Scoary2 associations between a pan-genome gene presence/absence matrix and numeric multi-omics traits and you must choose between strict FDR-BH filtering and a relaxed trait-wise Bonferroni correction to recover candidate genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-bonferroni-tuning))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-control**: Use when a pathway-level test yields one p-value per pathway across many tested pathways (e.g., NetGSA, DEGraph, CAMERA, Pathway-Express) and you must adjust for multiplicity with FDR q-values before calling pathways dysregulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-control))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when dcHiC has produced one raw chi-square p-value per genomic bin (from Mahalanobis distance on quantile-normalized compartment scores) or per candidate interaction, and you must call significant differential compartments or interactions at a controlled adjusted-p threshold such as FDR 10%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--ddb236b01abd8932))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when you have per-RBP constraint regressions (mean delta score vs. gnomAD allele-frequency bin) over Skipper enriched windows and must aggregate to RBP-transcript pairs and control the false discovery rate to nominate constrained transcripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--13899be922c430e8))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a CITRUS in silico knock out or association analysis yields one raw p-value per TF (hundreds of TFs tested simultaneously) and significant TF–alteration or TF-overlap associations must be controlled for multiple testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--91c614219c6ea0f6))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when testing many metabolite classes/subclasses simultaneously for overrepresentation among a subset of annotations (e.g. METASPACE-ML-exclusive hits) and you need multiplicity-controlled q-values rather than raw p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--b834dcd5bfc428b3))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when testing thousands of metabolic features or dozens of KEGG pathways for significance (e.g., volcano-plot differential testing with Log2FC &gt; 1.5, or hypergeometric MSEA), where uncorrected p-values would yield inflated false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--bf763270c7f749ed))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a limma linear model has been fit per metabolite across a large-scale metabolomics cohort (e.g. BioHEART-CT plasma samples) and you need adjusted p-values to call significant hypertension-associated metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--72e33b240cd22f48))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a taxa–SNP regression sweep (e.g., mbQTL linearTaxaSnp over microbeAbund × SnpFile) yields thousands of P values that must be corrected for multiple comparisons and diagnostic-checked before calling mbQTL associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--9e9c15c80e4f5c24))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a multivariable association analysis (e.g. MaAsLin2 on the FunOMIC-derived 2,679-sample abundance tables) tests many fungal taxa against many metadata variables and raw p-values must be corrected before calling significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--50a03865c8b9a9e0))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when running many parallel tests in the PreCanCell workflow — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--fab3c44f01d76aaf))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a per-gene test over hundreds/thousands of genes (mutation enrichment, DEG, or GSVA score difference) has produced raw p-values and you must report a defensible significant set, e.g. filtering mutation results with p &lt; 0.05 and FDR &lt; 0.01. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--0cf583974c0e609a))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when per-spot significance tests (e.g., Local Moran's I across many spots and gene signatures) yield thousands of unadjusted P-values, and hotspots must be declared only after multiple-testing correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--64c1f27f7fb2fb01))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a group-comparison task yields one statistical test per feature (e.g., one per cell type in a DCNet-inferred TCGA abundance matrix) and per-feature p-values must be adjusted for multiple testing before filtering by effect size and q-value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--88eef956988dfcda))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a differential expression test has been run across many genes (e.g. limma on a SummarizedExperiment of microarray intensities) and per-gene p-values must be converted to genome-wide FDR before thresholding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--2165dbdcb56f3849))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when a genome- or transcriptome-wide set of per-gene hypothesis tests (e.g., allelic burst-correlation logOR tests from SCALE) must be corrected for multiplicity before declaring significant genes at a fixed FDR level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--c1303b6197462fb5))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when an analysis tests many pathways (or pathway-by-alteration, pathway-by-drug, pathway-per-cohort combinations) simultaneously and a p-value family must be converted to FDR before declaring significance, e.g. the 10% FDR GDSC screen or pan-cancer driver/survival tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--c3fa4716e6130dce))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-correction**: Use when calling significant differential chromatin interactions from genome-wide scHi-C bin-pair test results, or when thresholding per-condition significance calls to build a set-difference baseline, so that thousands of simultaneous hypothesis tests are controlled with BH-adjusted p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-correction--468e27f700840a30))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-power-calculation**: Use when designing or evaluating a multi-omic differential-features study and you need a per-platform replicate number that meets target per-omic and average power under an FDR threshold, e.g. omicPower 0.6, averagePower 0.8, FDR 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-power-calculation))
- **multiple-testing-fdr-thresholding**: Use when thousands of paired hypothesis tests (e.g., per-gene edgeR likelihood-ratio tests on a gene-by-sample RNA-seq count matrix) must yield a defensible DE gene list via a raw-P plus FDR cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-fdr-thresholding))
- **multiple-testing-gptomics-bioskills**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/multiple-testing-gptomics-bioskills) · ★745)
- **multiple-testing-ledger**: Apply family-wise error and false-discovery control to a whole research programme, using the trial ledger's own registered count as m. TRIGGER - Bonferroni, Holm, Benjamini-Hochberg, Benjamini-Yekutieli, BHY, FDR, FWER, "adjust my p-values", statsmodels multipletests, "which of my factors survive the correction"; haircut Sharpe ratio, Harvey-Liu-Zhu, "t-stat of 3", factor zoo, "is a t of 2.1 enough"; "I screened 500 signals and 30 came back significant", "which correction should I use", "my strategies are all correlated so Bonferroni is too strict". SKIP for the deflated and probabilistic Sha… ([howard-lynn-ye/Fin-RSI](https://github.com/howard-lynn-ye/Fin-RSI/tree/HEAD/fin_skills/_skills/multiple-testing-ledger) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **multiple-testing-p-adjustment-selection**: Use when running methylR's Association Study downstream modules such as GO or pathway enrichment on a DMC results file and an explicit multiple-testing correction choice must be made through the graphical interface before statistics and plots are generated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-p-adjustment-selection))
- **multiple-testing-p-value-adjustment**: Use when you run univariate statistical tests (t-test, ANOVA, Mann-Whitney U, Kruskal-Wallis) over each metabolite/protein feature of a preprocessed feature matrix and need p-values adjusted for the large number of simultaneous tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-p-value-adjustment))
- **multiple-testing-padjust-correction**: Use when a metabolite abundance table (e.g. from Metabolomics Workbench study ST000915) has been tested per metabolite between two groups and raw p-values must be adjusted before calling significantly altered metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-padjust-correction))
- **multiple-testing-pvalue-adjustment**: Use when DAA results on pathway-level KEGG abundance tables contain raw p_val columns and significance must be judged on p_adjust, because hundreds of pathways are tested at once. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-pvalue-adjustment))
- **multiple-testing-pvalue-correction**: Use when a feature-by-sample quantification table (e.g., from MZmine2 with row ID, row m/z, row retention time, peak area) is annotated with bioassay values per LC-MS/MS fraction and per-feature correlation significance is needed before mapping onto molecular networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multiple-testing-pvalue-correction))
- **multiple-testing-pvalue-correction**: Use when a gene-level differential expression results table contains raw p-values from a negative-binomial or similar GLM fit and per-gene FDR-adjusted values (padj) are needed for downstream filtering or enrichment input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-pvalue-correction))
- **multiple-testing-pvalue-correction**: Use when calling RBP target transcripts from differential RNA-editing statistics where thousands of 50-nt transcript bins are tested simultaneously and raw Poisson P values would inflate false target calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-pvalue-correction))
- **multiple-testing-q-value-filtering**: Use when testing whether a specific mutational signature's per-sample contribution abundance differs between mutation-status groups of a gene (e.g. PIK3CA E545k mutated vs wild-type) in MAF-annotated tumor cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multiple-testing-q-value-filtering))
- **multiple-testing-qvalue-correction**: Use when a genome-wide scan (e.g., driverpower infer over eight test element sets: CDS, splice site, 5'-UTR, 3'-UTR, promoter, enhancer, lncRNA, lncRNA promoter) has produced raw p-values that must be converted to q-values before calling driver candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multiple-testing-qvalue-correction))
- **multiple-testing-small-panels**: Use when counting significant features after Bonferroni, Holm, BH, or BY correction, above all small counts (0, 1, 2) in qPCR, miRNA, or targeted panels; includes delta Ct basics and how to check a count holds. ([AdviktheGreat/Lumi_Virtual_Lab](https://github.com/AdviktheGreat/Lumi_Virtual_Lab/tree/HEAD/src/virtual_lab/builtin_skills/multiple-testing-small-panels) · ★2)
- **multiple_choice_qa_with_formatting**: Answer multiple-choice questions based on documents or general knowledge, strictly adhering to user-defined formatting constraints such as brevity or specific answer prefixes. ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/ConvSkill/english_gpt3.5_8_GLM4.7/multiple_choice_qa_with_formatting) · ★620 · 4 copies)
- **multiplechoice**: Use when the user asks for the decisions they need to make or confirm, what was decided on their behalf, or for options with your recommendation. Also use on your own when two or more open decisions or judgment calls block the work, or before long autonomous execution that depends on the user's choices. ([akasecurity/preflight-skills](https://github.com/akasecurity/preflight-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/multiplechoice) · ★1)
- **multiplet-detection**: Use when demultiplexing HTO-labeled pooled scRNA-seq droplets with demuxmix and droplets must be classified as singlets of a specific sample or as multiplets via posterior probabilities, with uncertain assignments discarded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiplet-detection))
- **multiplet-rate-estimation-from-snv-fraction**: Use when scRNA-seq data come from mixed cell populations (e.g. Jurkat/293T or 293T/3T3 mixing experiments, transplant chimerism samples) and an observed multiplet/doublet rate is needed from genotype rather than expression markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multiplet-rate-estimation-from-snv-fraction))
- **multiplet-rate-modeling-10x-genomics**: Use when planning or costing a 10X droplet scRNA-seq experiment with scPower and you must account for usable cells per lane given multiplet loss as a function of cells loaded per lane. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multiplet-rate-modeling-10x-genomics))
- **multipletesting-bonferroni-correction**: Use when many spatial correlation tests (e.g. Pearson tests over all parent–adduct ion pairs from massdiff objects) are run simultaneously and per-pair p-values must be thresholded to avoid false-positive adduct assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multipletesting-bonferroni-correction))
- **multiplex**: Orchestrate rasifiters-master work through the role-agent pipeline — scout → plan → plan-adversary → USER APPROVES → implementer(s) → impl-adversary → compile-check → git-version → USER tests on prod. Use for nontrivial changes, anything touching 3+ files or 2+ surfaces, parallel workstreams, or when the user says "multiplex"/"pipeline this"/"fan out". LIVING — append LESSONS_ARCHIVE.md on notable runs. ([vinaySankar2004/rasifiters-master](https://github.com/vinaySankar2004/rasifiters-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/multiplex) · ★1)
- **multiplex-imaging-analysis**: Optimizing segmentation feature extraction, SOM clustering, and spatial analysis for multiplex immunofluorescence imaging with cell compartment and ECM features ([smith6jt-cop/Skills_Registry](https://github.com/smith6jt-cop/Skills_Registry/tree/HEAD/plugins/training/multiplex-imaging-analysis/skills/multiplex-imaging-analysis) · ★4)
- **multiplex-imaging-feature-histogram-estimation**: Use when starting a UniFORM feature-level normalization run on single-cell MTI data (e.g., PRAD-CyCIF .pkl or .h5ad) and per-marker intensity distributions must be made comparable across batches prior to registration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multiplex-imaging-feature-histogram-estimation))
- **multiplex-imaging-intensity-normalization**: Use when integrating single-cell or pixel-level MTI data (CyCIF, ORION, COMET platforms) across batches with right-skewed, heterogeneous marker distributions that violate Z score/ComBat assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multiplex-imaging-intensity-normalization))
- **multiplex-network-construction**: Use when you have multiple edge-list files representing distinct relationship types (e.g. coexpression, PPI, regulation layers) and need a single multiplex network object with supra-adjacency and transition matrices for RWR analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiplex-network-construction))
- **multiplex-network-cross-validation**: Use when a constructed multiplex network needs statistical evaluation of whether curated gene sets (e.g., MAPMAN- or GO-derived) show topological similarity sufficient to recover held-out genes via random walk with restart. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiplex-network-cross-validation))
- **multiplex-network-gene-set-evaluation**: Use when a curated gene set (e.g. shared MAPMAN or GO annotation) must be validated for functional/topological connectivity in a preassembled multiplex network before using it for ranking or discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiplex-network-gene-set-evaluation))
- **multiplex-network-random-walk-scoring**: Use when you have a set of seed genes (e.g. from a pathway or GWAS) and a preassembled or custom multiplex network (multiple omic layers), and need ranked topological context around the seeds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiplex-network-random-walk-scoring))
- **multiplex-network-shortest-path-extraction**: Use when you have a prebuilt multiplex network (RData) and two gene-set files, and you need the concrete shortest paths and path lengths connecting every source-target gene pair. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiplex-network-shortest-path-extraction))
- **multiplex-network-statistics-extraction**: Use when a multiplex network object (.Rdata from RWR_make_multiplex or a preassembled multiplex) needs characterization: per-layer node/edge counts and between-layer edge overlap (Jaccard) before running RWR analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multiplex-network-statistics-extraction))
- **multiplexed-10x-sample-read-reassignment**: Use when 10x Genomics droplet libraries from multiple samples were sequenced together on a patterned-flowcell machine (HiSeq 4000, HiSeq X, NovaSeq) and barcode swapping between samples must be corrected at the molecule level. - **multiverse-analysis**: Use this skill after confound-audit and before any conclusion is drawn from the analysis. Required for medium and high-stakes analyses; recommended always. Addresses the garden-of-forking-paths problem — every analytical choice (segmentation, outlier rule, time window, transformation, model) could have plausibly gone differently, and a result that holds on only one path is fragile. Logs every choice with rationale and alternatives considered, runs alternative paths where feasible, and flags fragile results that don't hold up across the multiverse.
